    FROM [MangaRating] AS [m0]

    WHEN [t].[ParentId] IS NULL THEN 0
    ELSE [t].[ViewCount]
END AS bigint) AS [View]
FROM [Manga] AS [m]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c].[ParentId], [c].[MangaId], [c].[ViewCount]
    FROM [Counting] AS [c]
    WHERE ([c].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [c].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [c].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t] ON [m].[Id] = [t].[MangaId]
WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL

    WHEN [t].[ParentId] IS NULL THEN 0
    ELSE [t].[ViewCount]
